// ==========================================
// FILE: src/lib/url.ts
// ==========================================

/**
 * URL Sanitizer to prevent XSS and malicious javascript: or data: URIs.
 * Only http and https protocols are permitted.
 */
export function sanitizeHttpUrl(rawUrl: string | null | undefined): string | null {
  if (!rawUrl || typeof rawUrl !== "string") {
    return null;
  }

  const trimmed = rawUrl.trim();
  if (!trimmed) {
    return null;
  }

  try {
    const parsed = new URL(trimmed);
    if (parsed.protocol === "http:" || parsed.protocol === "https:") {
      return parsed.toString();
    }
    return null;
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}


// ==========================================
// FILE: src/server/indicators.ts
// ==========================================

import fs from "node:fs";
import path from "node:path";
import {
  IndicatorSchema,
  type Indicator,
  type DirectoryIndexItem,
} from "@/domain/indicator.schema";

const CONTENT_DIR = path.join(process.cwd(), "content", "indicators");
const GENERATED_INDEX_PATH = path.join(process.cwd(), "generated", "directory-index.json");

/**
 * Server-only repository layer for indicators.
 */
export async function getDirectoryIndex(): Promise<DirectoryIndexItem[]> {
  try {
    if (fs.existsSync(GENERATED_INDEX_PATH)) {
      const content = fs.readFileSync(GENERATED_INDEX_PATH, "utf-8");
      return JSON.parse(content) as DirectoryIndexItem[];
    }
  } catch (err) {
    console.error("[getDirectoryIndex] Error reading directory index:", err);
  }

  return [];
}

export async function getIndicatorBySlug(slug: string): Promise<Indicator | null> {
  if (!slug || slug === "_none" || !/^[a-z0-9-]+$/.test(slug)) {
    return null;
  }

  try {
    const filePath = path.join(CONTENT_DIR, `${slug}.json`);
    if (!fs.existsSync(filePath)) {
      return null;
    }

    const content = fs.readFileSync(filePath, "utf-8");
    const json = JSON.parse(content);
    const result = IndicatorSchema.safeParse(json);
    if (!result.success) {
      console.error(`[getIndicatorBySlug] Invalid schema for indicator ${slug}:`, result.error);
      return null;
    }

    return result.data;
  } catch (err) {
    console.error(`[getIndicatorBySlug] Failed to load indicator ${slug}:`, err);
    return null;
  }
}

export async function getAllIndicatorSlugs(): Promise<string[]> {
  try {
    if (fs.existsSync(CONTENT_DIR)) {
      const files = fs.readdirSync(CONTENT_DIR).filter((f) => f.endsWith(".json"));
      return files.map((f) => f.replace(/\.json$/, ""));
    }
  } catch (err) {
    console.error("[getAllIndicatorSlugs] Error listing indicator files:", err);
  }
  return [];
}


// ==========================================
// FILE: src/server/page-factories.tsx
// ==========================================

import React from "react";
import Link from "next/link";
import { notFound } from "next/navigation";
import type { Metadata } from "next";
import { ArrowLeft, Tag } from "lucide-react";
import type { Locale } from "@/domain/indicator.schema";
import { getDirectoryIndex, getIndicatorBySlug, getAllIndicatorSlugs } from "./indicators";
import { routes, CATEGORY_LABELS, t } from "@/lib/i18n";
import DirectoryExplorer from "@/components/directory/DirectoryExplorer";
import { IndicatorVisualization } from "@/components/visualizations/IndicatorVisualization";
import SourceCard from "@/components/detail/SourceCard";
import MethodologicalNotes from "@/components/detail/MethodologicalNotes";

export async function renderHomePage(locale: Locale) {
  const initialItems = await getDirectoryIndex();
  return <DirectoryExplorer initialItems={initialItems} locale={locale} />;
}

export async function generateIndicatorStaticParams() {
  const slugs = await getAllIndicatorSlugs();
  return slugs.map((slug) => ({ slug }));
}

export async function generateIndicatorMetadata(
  locale: Locale,
  slug: string
): Promise<Metadata> {
  const indicator = await getIndicatorBySlug(slug);
  if (!indicator) {
    return {
      title: locale === "en" ? "Indicator Not Found | Factor.es" : "Indicador no encontrado | Factor.es",
    };
  }

  const question = indicator.question[locale];
  const summary = indicator.contextNotes[locale][0] ?? question;

  return {
    title: `${question} | Factor.es`,
    description: summary,
    openGraph: {
      title: `${question} | Factor.es`,
      description: summary,
    },
  };
}

export async function renderIndicatorPage(locale: Locale, slug: string) {
  const indicator = await getIndicatorBySlug(slug);
  if (!indicator) {
    notFound();
  }

  const question = indicator.question[locale];
  const categoryLabel = CATEGORY_LABELS[indicator.category]?.[locale] ?? indicator.category;
  const backHref = routes.home(locale);

  return (
    <article className="space-y-6 animate-in fade-in duration-200">
      {/* Back to Directory Button */}
      <div>
        <Link
          href={backHref}
          className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-semibold text-slate-300 hover:text-white bg-slate-900 hover:bg-slate-800 border border-slate-800 px-3.5 py-2 rounded-full transition-colors shadow-xs"
        >
          <ArrowLeft className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
          <span>{t("backToExplorer", locale)}</span>
        </Link>
      </div>

      {/* Category Badge */}
      <div>
        <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1 bg-blue-950 text-blue-300 border border-blue-800/80 rounded-lg text-xs font-semibold">
          <Tag className="w-3 h-3 text-blue-400" />
          {categoryLabel}
        </span>
      </div>

      {/* H1 Question Header */}
      <header className="space-y-2">
        <h1 className="text-xl sm:text-2xl font-bold text-white leading-snug tracking-tight">
          {question}
        </h1>
      </header>

      {/* Interactive Visualization (Kind Dispatched) */}
      <IndicatorVisualization indicator={indicator} locale={locale} />

      {/* Methodological Context Notes */}
      <MethodologicalNotes notes={indicator.contextNotes[locale]} locale={locale} />

      {/* Official Data Source & Traceability */}
      <SourceCard
        sources={indicator.sources}
        lastUpdated={indicator.lastUpdated}
        locale={locale}
      />
    </article>
  );
}


// ==========================================
// FILE: tests/classify-choropleth.test.ts
// ==========================================

import { describe, it, expect } from "vitest";
import { classifyChoropleth } from "../src/components/visualizations/immigration/jenks";

const TEST_PALETTE = ["#0284c7", "#2563eb", "#7c3aed", "#c026d3", "#db2777"] as const;

describe("classifyChoropleth Algorithm", () => {
  it("handles empty arrays gracefully", () => {
    const result = classifyChoropleth([], TEST_PALETTE);
    expect(result.classes.length).toBe(0);
    expect(result.minVal).toBe(0);
    expect(result.maxVal).toBe(0);
    expect(result.getClass(50)).toBeNull();
    expect(result.getColor(50, "#fallback")).toBe("#fallback");
  });

  it("handles arrays with only 0 or negative values", () => {
    const result = classifyChoropleth([0, -1, -5], TEST_PALETTE);
    expect(result.classes.length).toBe(0);
    expect(result.getClass(0)).toBeNull();
  });

  it("handles single item array", () => {
    const result = classifyChoropleth([42], TEST_PALETTE);
    expect(result.classes.length).toBe(1);
    expect(result.minVal).toBe(42);
    expect(result.maxVal).toBe(42);
    expect(result.classes[0].color).toBe(TEST_PALETTE[0]);
    expect(result.getColor(42)).toBe(TEST_PALETTE[0]);
  });

  it("handles multiple identical values without crashing or empty cuts", () => {
    const result = classifyChoropleth([100, 100, 100, 100], TEST_PALETTE);
    expect(result.classes.length).toBe(1);
    expect(result.minVal).toBe(100);
    expect(result.maxVal).toBe(100);
    expect(result.getColor(100)).toBe(TEST_PALETTE[0]);
  });

  it("gracefully degrades when fewer unique values exist than palette length", () => {
    const result = classifyChoropleth([10, 50], TEST_PALETTE);
    expect(result.classes.length).toBe(2);
    expect(result.minVal).toBe(10);
    expect(result.maxVal).toBe(50);
    expect(result.classes[0].min).toBe(10);
    expect(result.classes[0].max).toBe(10);
    expect(result.classes[1].max).toBe(50);
    expect(result.getColor(10)).toBe(TEST_PALETTE[0]);
    expect(result.getColor(50)).toBe(TEST_PALETTE[1]);
  });

  it("handles standard skewed distribution (e.g. immigration volumes) and assigns contiguous classes", () => {
    const skewedValues = [
      120, 150, 200, 310, 450, 800, 1200, 1500, 2400, 4500, 12000, 35000, 120000,
    ];
    const result = classifyChoropleth(skewedValues, TEST_PALETTE);

    expect(result.classes.length).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(2);
    expect(result.classes.length).toBeLessThanOrEqual(TEST_PALETTE.length);
    expect(result.minVal).toBe(120);
    expect(result.maxVal).toBe(120000);

    // Verify classes are contiguous
    for (let i = 1; i < result.classes.length; i++) {
      expect(result.classes[i].min).toBe(result.classes[i - 1].max);
      expect(result.classes[i].max).toBeGreaterThan(result.classes[i].min);
    }

    // Verify lowest and highest values get classified
    const lowestColor = result.getColor(120);
    const highestColor = result.getColor(120000);
    expect(lowestColor).toBe(TEST_PALETTE[0]);
    expect(highestColor).toBe(result.classes[result.classes.length - 1].color);
  });
});


// ==========================================
// FILE: tests/data-integrity.test.ts
// ==========================================

import { describe, it, expect } from "vitest";
import fs from "node:fs";
import path from "node:path";
import { IndicatorSchema } from "../src/domain/indicator.schema";

const INDICATORS_DIR = path.join(process.cwd(), "content", "indicators");

describe("Data Integrity & Invariants", () => {
  const files = fs.readdirSync(INDICATORS_DIR).filter((f) => f.endsWith(".json"));

  it("should have at least one indicator file", () => {
    expect(files.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
  });

  files.forEach((file) => {
    describe(`Indicator: ${file}`, () => {
      const filePath = path.join(INDICATORS_DIR, file);
      const raw = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(filePath, "utf-8"));

      it("conforms strictly to IndicatorSchema", () => {
        const result = IndicatorSchema.safeParse(raw);
        if (!result.success) {
          console.error(`Validation errors in ${file}:`, result.error.format());
        }
        expect(result.success).toBe(true);
      });

      if (raw.kind === "immigration-flow") {
        it("reconciles immigration numbers: sum(byCountryIso2) + sum(residualByRegion) === declaredTotal", () => {
          for (const yearData of raw.years) {
            const countrySum = Object.values(yearData.byCountryIso2 as Record<string, number>).reduce(
              (acc, v) => acc + v,
              0
            );
            const residualSum = Object.values(
              yearData.residualByRegion as Record<string, number>
            ).reduce((acc, v) => acc + v, 0);
            const totalSum = countrySum + residualSum;

            expect(Math.abs(totalSum - yearData.declaredTotal)).toBeLessThanOrEqual(2);
          }
        });
      }

      if (raw.kind === "fiscal-balance") {
        it("reconciles fiscal figures: sum(stateRevenue) ≈ totalRevenue and sum(stateSpending) ≈ totalSpending", () => {
          for (const point of raw.points) {
            const revSum = Object.values(point.stateRevenueMmEur as Record<string, number>).reduce(
              (acc, v) => acc + v,
              0
            );
            const spendSum = Object.values(point.stateSpendingMmEur as Record<string, number>).reduce(
              (acc, v) => acc + v,
              0
            );

            expect(Math.abs(revSum - point.totalRevenueMmEur)).toBeLessThanOrEqual(0.1);
            expect(Math.abs(spendSum - point.totalSpendingMmEur)).toBeLessThanOrEqual(0.1);
            expect(
              Math.abs(point.totalRevenueMmEur - point.totalSpendingMmEur - point.deficitMmEur)
            ).toBeLessThanOrEqual(0.1);
          }
        });
      }
    });
  });
});


// ==========================================
// FILE: tests/format.test.ts
// ==========================================

import { describe, it, expect } from "vitest";
import {
  formatNumber,
  formatCompactNumber,
  formatPercent,
  formatCurrency,
  formatIndicatorValue,
} from "../src/lib/format";

describe("Locale-Aware Number Formatters", () => {
  describe("formatNumber", () => {
    it("formats thousands separator in Spanish (period)", () => {
      expect(formatNumber(1250000, "es")).toBe("1.250.000");
    });

    it("formats thousands separator in English (comma)", () => {
      expect(formatNumber(1250000, "en")).toBe("1,250,000");
    });

    it("handles 0 and non-finite values safely", () => {
      expect(formatNumber(0, "es")).toBe("0");
      expect(formatNumber(NaN, "es")).toBe("0");
      expect(formatNumber(Infinity, "es")).toBe("0");
    });
  });

  describe("formatCompactNumber", () => {
    it("formats compact notation in Spanish", () => {
      expect(formatCompactNumber(500, "es")).toBe("500");
      expect(formatCompactNumber(1500, "es")).toBe("2 mil");
      expect(formatCompactNumber(1500000, "es")).toBe("1,5 M");
      expect(formatCompactNumber(2500000000, "es")).toBe("2,5 MM");
    });

    it("formats compact notation in English", () => {
      expect(formatCompactNumber(500, "en")).toBe("500");
      expect(formatCompactNumber(1500, "en")).toBe("1.5K");
      expect(formatCompactNumber(1500000, "en")).toBe("1.5M");
      expect(formatCompactNumber(2500000000, "en")).toBe("2.5B");
    });
  });

  describe("formatPercent", () => {
    it("formats percentages with 1 decimal by default", () => {
      expect(formatPercent(187.0, "es")).toBe("187,0%");
      expect(formatPercent(187.0, "en")).toBe("187.0%");
    });

    it("formats percentages with custom decimal places", () => {
      expect(formatPercent(45.678, "es", 2)).toBe("45,68%");
      expect(formatPercent(45.678, "en", 2)).toBe("45.68%");
    });
  });

  describe("formatCurrency", () => {
    it("formats currency in EUR", () => {
      expect(formatCurrency(1500, "es", "eur")).toBe("1.500 €");
      expect(formatCurrency(1500, "en", "eur")).toBe("1,500 €");
    });

    it("formats currency in MM€", () => {
      expect(formatCurrency(661.5, "es", "mmeur")).toBe("661,5 MM€");
      expect(formatCurrency(661.5, "en", "mmeur")).toBe("661.5 MM€");
    });
  });

  describe("formatIndicatorValue", () => {
    it("formats according to ValueFormat enum", () => {
      expect(formatIndicatorValue(187, "percent", "es")).toBe("187,0%");
      expect(formatIndicatorValue(1288562, "number", "es", false)).toBe("1.288.562");
      expect(formatIndicatorValue(1288562, "number", "es", true)).toBe("1,3 M");
    });
  });
});


// ==========================================
// FILE: tests/sanitize-url.test.ts
// ==========================================

import { describe, it, expect } from "vitest";
import { sanitizeHttpUrl } from "../src/lib/url";

describe("sanitizeHttpUrl Security Function", () => {
  it("permits standard https:// URLs", () => {
    const url = "https://www.ine.es/jaxiT3/Tabla.htm?t=56951";
    expect(sanitizeHttpUrl(url)).toBe(url);
  });

  it("permits standard http:// URLs", () => {
    const url = "http://datos.gob.es/catalogo";
    expect(sanitizeHttpUrl(url)).toBe(url);
  });

  it("blocks javascript: XSS attack vectors", () => {
    expect(sanitizeHttpUrl("javascript:alert(1)")).toBeNull();
    expect(sanitizeHttpUrl("JAVASCRIPT:document.location='http://evil.com'")).toBeNull();
  });

  it("blocks data: URI attack vectors", () => {
    expect(sanitizeHttpUrl("data:text/html,<script>alert(1)</script>")).toBeNull();
  });

  it("blocks file: and vbscript: URIs", () => {
    expect(sanitizeHttpUrl("file:///etc/passwd")).toBeNull();
    expect(sanitizeHttpUrl("vbscript:msgbox(1)")).toBeNull();
  });

  it("handles null, undefined, whitespace, and non-URL strings safely", () => {
    expect(sanitizeHttpUrl(null)).toBeNull();
    expect(sanitizeHttpUrl(undefined)).toBeNull();
    expect(sanitizeHttpUrl("")).toBeNull();
    expect(sanitizeHttpUrl("   ")).toBeNull();
    expect(sanitizeHttpUrl("not a valid url")).toBeNull();
  });
});

// ==========================================
// FILE: types/index.ts
// ==========================================

export * from "@/src/domain/indicator.schema";

export const CATEGORIES = [
  "Todas",
  "Vivienda",
  "Economía y Empleo",
  "Sanidad",
  "Pensiones y Demografía",
] as const;

export type Category = (typeof CATEGORIES)[number];

export type ChartType = "line" | "bar";

export interface DataPoint {
  year: string;
  value: number;
  date?: string;
}

export type MetricSeriesPoint = DataPoint;

export interface SourceInfo {
  institution: string;
  title: string;
  url: string;
  shortName?: string;
  tableId?: string;
  period?: string;
  lastUpdated?: string;
}

export type MetricSource = SourceInfo;

export interface TraceableSource {
  sources?: SourceInfo[];
  source?: SourceInfo;
}

export interface BaseEntityItem {
  id: string;
  category: Category;
  question: string;
  unit: string;
  name?: string;
  state?: string;
}

export interface DatoItem extends BaseEntityItem, TraceableSource {
  chartType: ChartType;
  dataPoints: DataPoint[];
  contextNotes: string[];
  last_updated?: string;
  metrics?: Record<string, any>;
}

export type CountyDetail = DatoItem;

export interface ItemIndexItem extends BaseEntityItem {
  latestValue: number;
  latestYear: string;
  lastUpdated: string;
  latest_summary?: Record<string, number>;
}

export type CountyIndexItem = ItemIndexItem;

export interface MetricMeta {
  title: string;
  unit: string;
  description?: string;
  source?: MetricSource;
}

export interface GlobalMetadata {
  last_updated: string;
  metrics?: Record<string, MetricMeta>;
  categories?: Category[];
  total_items?: number;
}

// Specific domain types for Immigration series
export interface RegionalBlockData {
  total: number;
  countries: Record<string, number>;
}

export interface AnnualBlock {
  year: string;
  total: number;
  blocks: Record<string, RegionalBlockData>;
}

export interface ImmigrationItem extends TraceableSource {
  id: string;
  category?: Category | string;
  question?: string;
  unit?: string;
  blocks: Array<{
    id: string;
    name: string;
    color: string;
    countries: Array<{ id: string; name: string }>;
  }>;
  annualBlocks: AnnualBlock[];
}
